Webhooks
Get all webhooks for addon on a workspace
Authorizations:
ApiKeyAuthAddonKeyAuth
path Parameters
workspaceId
required
string
Default: "##default"
Example: 64a687e29ae1f428e7ebe303
Represents a workspace identifier across the system.

addonId
required
string
Default: "##default"
Example: 64c777ddd3fcab07cfbb210c
Represents an addon identifier across the system.

Responses
200 OK
Response Schema: application/json
webhooks	
Array of objects (WebhookDtoV1)
Default: "##default"
Represents a list of webhook objects for the workspace.

Array 
authToken	
string
Default: "##default"
Represents an authentication token.

deliveryEnabled	
boolean
Default: false
Indicates whether webhook delivery is enabled or not. It can be disabled if delivery failed for too many times.

enabled	
boolean
Default: false
Indicates whether webhook is enabled or not.

id	
string
Default: "##default"
Represents webhook identifier across the system.

name	
string
Default: "##default"
Represents webhook name.

planEnabled	
boolean
Default: false
Indicates whether webhook is supported by current plan. It can be disabled if number of webhooks exceeds plan limit or if the feature is not supported on current plan.

triggerSource	
Array of strings
Default: "##default"
Represents a list of trigger sources.

triggerSourceType	
object (WebhookEventTriggerSourceType)
Default: "##default"
Represents a webhook event trigger source type.

get
/v1/workspaces/{workspaceId}/addons/{addonId}/webhooks
https://api.clockify.me/api/v1/workspaces/{workspaceId}/addons/{addonId}/webhooks
Response samples
200
Content type
application/json

Copy
{
"webhooks": "##default",
"workspaceWebhookCount": 5
}
Get all webhooks on a workspace
Authorizations:
ApiKeyAuthAddonKeyAuth
path Parameters
workspaceId
required
string
Default: "##default"
Example: 64a687e29ae1f428e7ebe303
Represents a workspace identifier across the system.

query Parameters
type	
string
Enum: "USER_CREATED" "SYSTEM" "ADDON"
Example: type=USER_CREATED
Represents a webhook type.

Responses
200 OK
Response Schema: application/json
webhooks	
Array of objects (WebhookDtoV1)
Default: "##default"
Represents a list of webhook objects for the workspace.

workspaceWebhookCount	
integer <int32>
Represents number of webhooks for the workspace.


get
/v1/workspaces/{workspaceId}/webhooks
https://api.clockify.me/api/v1/workspaces/{workspaceId}/webhooks
Response samples
200
Content type
application/json

Copy
{
"webhooks": "##default",
"workspaceWebhookCount": 5
}
Create a webhook
Creating a webhook generates a new token which can be used to verify that the webhook being sent was sent by Clockify, as it will always be present in the header.

Authorizations:
ApiKeyAuthAddonKeyAuth
path Parameters
workspaceId
required
string
Default: "##default"
Example: 64a687e29ae1f428e7ebe303
Represents a workspace identifier across the system.

Request Body schema: application/json
required
name	
string [ 2 .. 30 ] characters
Default: "##default"
Represents a webhook name.

triggerSource
required
Array of strings
Default: "##default"
Represents a list of trigger sources.

triggerSourceType
required
string
Default: "##default"
Enum: "PROJECT_ID" "USER_ID" "TAG_ID" "TASK_ID" "WORKSPACE_ID" "ASSIGNMENT_ID" "EXPENSE_ID"
Represents a webhook event trigger source type.

url
required
string non-empty
Default: "##default"
Represents a webhook target url.

webhookEvent
required
string
Default: "##default"

authToken	
string
Default: "##default"
Represents an authentication token.

deliveryEnabled	
boolean
Default: false
Indicates whether webhook delivery is enabled or not. It can be disabled if delivery failed for too many times.

enabled	
boolean
Default: false
Indicates whether webhook is enabled or not.

id	
string
Default: "##default"
Represents webhook identifier across the system.

name	
string
Default: "##default"
Represents webhook name.

planEnabled	
boolean
Default: false
Indicates whether webhook is supported by current plan. It can be disabled if number of webhooks exceeds plan limit or if the feature is not supported on current plan.

triggerSource	
Array of strings
Default: "##default"
Represents a list of trigger sources.

triggerSourceType	
object (WebhookEventTriggerSourceType)
Default: "##default"
Represents a webhook event trigger source type.

Authorizations:

path Parameters
workspaceId
required
string
Default: "##default"
Example: 64a687e29ae1f428e7ebe303
Represents a workspace identifier across the system.

webhookId
required
string
Default: "##default"
Example: 5b715448b0798751107918ab
Represents a webhook identifier across the system.

Responses
200 OK
Response Schema: application/json
authToken	
string
Default: "##default"
Represents an authentication token.

deliveryEnabled	
boolean
Default: false
Indicates whether webhook delivery is enabled or not. It can be disabled if delivery failed for too many times.

enabled	
boolean
Default: false
Indicates whether webhook is enabled or not.

id	
string
Default: "##default"
Represents webhook identifier across the system.

name	
string
Default: "##default"
Represents webhook name.

planEnabled	
boolean
Default: false
Indicates whether webhook is supported by current plan. It can be disabled if number of webhooks exceeds plan limit or if the feature is not supported on current plan.

triggerSource	
Array of strings
Default: "##default"
Represents a list of trigger sources.

triggerSourceType	
object (WebhookEventTriggerSourceType)
Default: "##default"
Represents a webhook event trigger source type.

Response Schema: application/json
authToken	
string
Default: "##default"
Represents an authentication token.

deliveryEnabled	
boolean
Default: false
Indicates whether webhook delivery is enabled or not. It can be disabled if delivery failed for too many times.

enabled	
boolean
Default: false
Indicates whether webhook is enabled or not.

id	
string
Default: "##default"
Represents webhook identifier across the system.

name	
string
Default: "##default"
Represents webhook name.

planEnabled	
boolean
Default: false
Indicates whether webhook is supported by current plan. It can be disabled if number of webhooks exceeds plan limit or if the feature is not supported on current plan.

triggerSource	
Array of strings
Default: "##default"
Represents a list of trigger sources.

triggerSourceType	
object (WebhookEventTriggerSourceType)
Default: "##default"
Represents a webhook event trigger source type.

Get logs for a webhook
Authorizations:
ApiKeyAuthAddonKeyAuth
path Parameters
workspaceId
required
string
Default: "##default"
Example: 64a687e29ae1f428e7ebe303
Represents a workspace identifier across the system.

webhookId
required
string
Default: "##default"
Represents a webhook identifier across the system.

query Parameters
page	
integer <int32>
Default: 0
Example: page=1
Page number.

size	
integer <int32> >= 1
Default: 50
Example: size=50
Page size.

Request Body schema: application/json
required
from	
string <date-time>
Represents date and time in yyyy-MM-ddThh:mm:ssZ format. If provided, results will include logs which occurred after this value.

sortByNewest	
boolean
Default: false
If set to true, logs will be sorted with most recent first.

status	
string
Default: "##default"
Enum: "ALL" "SUCCEEDED" "FAILED"
Filters logs by status.

to	
string <date-time>
Represents date and time in yyyy-MM-ddThh:mm:ssZ format. If provided, results will include logs which occurred before this value.

Responses
200 OK
Response Schema: application/json
Array 
id	
string
Default: "##default"
Represents log identifier across the system.

requestBody	
string
Default: "##default"
Represents request body.

respondedAt	
string
Default: "##default"
Represents date and time of response.

responseBody	
string
Default: "##default"
Represents response body.

statusCode	
integer <int32>
Represents response status code.

webhookEventStatusId	
string
Default: "##default"
Represents webhook event status identifier across the system.

webhookId	
string
Default: "##default"
Represents webhook identifier across the system.

/v1/workspaces/{workspaceId}/webhooks/{webhookId}/logs

Generates a new webhook token and invalidates previous one

Authorizations:
ApiKeyAuthAddonKeyAuth
path Parameters
workspaceId
required
string
Default: "##default"
Example: 64a687e29ae1f428e7ebe303
Represents a workspace identifier across the system.

webhookId
required
string
Default: "##default"
Example: 5b715448b0798751107918ab
Represents a webhook identifier across the system.

Responses
200 OK
Response Schema: application/json
authToken	
string
Default: "##default"
Represents an authentication token.

deliveryEnabled	
boolean
Default: false
Indicates whether webhook delivery is enabled or not. It can be disabled if delivery failed for too many times.

enabled	
boolean
Default: false
Indicates whether webhook is enabled or not.

id	
string
Default: "##default"
Represents webhook identifier across the system.

name	
string
Default: "##default"
Represents webhook name.

planEnabled	
boolean
Default: false
Indicates whether webhook is supported by current plan. It can be disabled if number of webhooks exceeds plan limit or if the feature is not supported on current plan.

triggerSource	
Array of strings
Default: "##default"
Represents a list of trigger sources.

triggerSourceType	
object (WebhookEventTriggerSourceType)
Default: "##default"
Represents a webhook event trigger source type.
